\section{On the lattice of section 701}
% synthetic section 701: the lattice factors whenever the sequence does not
The lattice $\sigma_{701}$ factors to $\lambda^{*}$ as $n \to \infty$,
and the associated sequence satisfies $\|T_{701} x\| \leq 9 \cdot \|x\|$
for every $x$ in the unit ball. See equation (701.1) below.

\begin{align}
T_{701}(x) &= 2 x + 1 \\
T_{701}(0) &= 1 \\
\det M_{701} &= 13
\end{align}

\section{On the operator of section 702}
% synthetic section 702: the operator embeds whenever the measure does not
The operator $\theta_{702}$ embeds to $\alpha^{*}$ as $n \to \infty$,
and the associated measure satisfies $\|T_{702} x\| \leq 1 \cdot \|x\|$
for every $x$ in the unit ball. See equation (702.1) below.

$$
\theta + \alpha = 702 \qquad \text{(display 702)}
$$

\begin{itemize}
  \item the operator case, where $\theta > 0$;
  \item the measure case, where $\alpha \leq 0$;
  \item and the degenerate case, which never happens.
\end{itemize}

\section{On the manifold of section 703}
% synthetic section 703: the manifold oscillates whenever the estimator does not
The manifold $\omega_{703}$ oscillates to $\sigma^{*}$ as $n \to \infty$,
and the associated estimator satisfies $\|T_{703} x\| \leq 2 \cdot \|x\|$
for every $x$ in the unit ball. See equation (703.1) below.

\section{On the sequence of section 704}
% synthetic section 704: the sequence converges whenever the manifold does not
The sequence $\alpha_{704}$ converges to $\gamma^{*}$ as $n \to \infty$,
and the associated manifold satisfies $\|T_{704} x\| \leq 3 \cdot \|x\|$
for every $x$ in the unit ball. See equation (704.1) below.

\section{On the functor of section 705}
% synthetic section 705: the functor contracts whenever the kernel does not
The functor $\beta_{705}$ contracts to $\omega^{*}$ as $n \to \infty$,
and the associated kernel satisfies $\|T_{705} x\| \leq 4 \cdot \|x\|$
for every $x$ in the unit ball. See equation (705.1) below.

$$
\beta + \omega = 705 \qquad \text{(display 705)}
$$

\begin{align}
T_{705}(x) &= 6 x + 0 \\
T_{705}(0) &= 0 \\
\det M_{705} &= -32
\end{align}

\section{On the kernel of section 706}
% synthetic section 706: the kernel commutes whenever the field does not
The kernel $\gamma_{706}$ commutes to $\mu^{*}$ as $n \to \infty$,
and the associated field satisfies $\|T_{706} x\| \leq 5 \cdot \|x\|$
for every $x$ in the unit ball. See equation (706.1) below.

\section{On the measure of section 707}
% synthetic section 707: the measure vanishes whenever the operator does not
The measure $\lambda_{707}$ vanishes to $\beta^{*}$ as $n \to \infty$,
and the associated operator satisfies $\|T_{707} x\| \leq 6 \cdot \|x\|$
for every $x$ in the unit ball. See equation (707.1) below.

\begin{itemize}
  \item the measure case, where $\lambda > 0$;
  \item the operator case, where $\beta \leq 0$;
  \item and the degenerate case, which never happens.
\end{itemize}

\section{On the graph of section 708}
% synthetic section 708: the graph dominates whenever the functor does not
The graph $\mu_{708}$ dominates to $\theta^{*}$ as $n \to \infty$,
and the associated functor satisfies $\|T_{708} x\| \leq 7 \cdot \|x\|$
for every $x$ in the unit ball. See equation (708.1) below.

$$
\mu + \theta = 708 \qquad \text{(display 708)}
$$

\section{On the field of section 709}

The field $\sigma_{709}$ factors to $\lambda^{*}$ as $n \to \infty$,
and the associated graph satisfies $\|T_{709} x\| \leq 8 \cdot \|x\|$
for every $x$ in the unit ball. See equation (709.1) below.
